        7: failure): this one, ``iip.enterprise.orchestrator.EnterpriseOrchestrator``,

        9: 
       10: This module and ``EnterpriseOrchestrator`` model the *same* concept
       11: (SOURCE/DISCOVERY -> ATLAS -> KNOWLEDGE -> INTELLIGENCE -> DECISION ->
       12: VALIDATION -> export/reporting) â€” true duplicates. This one is
       13: canonical between the two: it is the more complete of the pair (tracks
       14: ``run_id``, threads a ``context`` dict through every stage, and
>>     15: accumulates per-stage evidence via ``SystemArtifact.evidence_ids``).
       16: ``EnterpriseOrchestrator`` is documented as the legacy variant of this
       17: same concept â€” do not delegate between them, their handler call
       18: signatures are incompatible and each is locked in by its own test
       19: suite.
       20: 
       21: ``PortfolioCycleOrchestrator`` is NOT a duplicate of this one, despite
       22: the superficial shape match â€” it models a different concept (a
       23: recurring cycle over an *already onboarded* portfolio, starting from
